Reuse image 3a47b757 from build 41729881283 and run one ordinary Firmus PP4 serving job. Rounds 32 and 33 measured a pooled twelve-GPU saving of about 16 to 25 microseconds per MegaMoE call with full byte equality and endurance, but the HEAD gate processes did not exit within the deadline after publishing healthy results, so the selection rejected the candidate both times.
